        _lines.setValue(CollectionsKt.emptyList());
    }

    public final synchronized String dump() {
        return CollectionsKt.joinToString$default(buffer, "\n", null, null, 0, null, null, 62, null);
    }

    public final String maskToken(String token) {
        Intrinsics.checkNotNullParameter(token, "token");
        String t = StringsKt.trim((CharSequence) StringsKt.removePrefix(token, (CharSequence) "Bearer ")).toString();
        if (t.length() <= 6) {
            return "****";
        }
        return "****" + StringsKt.takeLast(t, 6) + " (len=" + t.length() + ")";
    }
}
